### [150] [PASS: Perturbation augmented space group structure sampling for transferable Fe-O machine learning interatomic potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28000)
*Zixiong Wei,Fei Shuang,Poulumi Dey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PASS（Perturbation Augmented Space group structure Sampling）的方法，用于生成高代表性的小尺寸第一性原理数据集，并基于原子团簇展开（ACE）框架开发了首个可迁移的Fe-O体系机器学习原子间势（MLIP），成功应用于大规模Fe氧化模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe-O体系具有复杂的结构和磁性特征，传统方法难以生成高质量的第一性原理数据集来训练可靠的原子间势；需要一种系统性的方法来生成广泛且有代表性的数据集，从而开发准确且可迁移的机器学习原子间势。

Method: 提出了PASS方法，通过扰动增强和空间群结构采样生成少于10个原子的小尺寸胞数据集；基于该数据集，采用原子团簇展开（ACE）框架训练了首个可迁移的Fe-O体系机器学习原子间势（MLIP）。

Result: 在纯Fe和Fe-O体系的体相、表面和界面性质上全面验证了ACE MLIP的准确性和能力；展示了使用该MLIP在大规模Fe氧化模拟中形成FeO类似结构的成果，证明其能捕捉氧化物生长的反应复杂性，同时对扩展体系保持计算可行性。

Conclusion: PASS方法能够生成准确且可迁移的机器学习原子间势，该势函数具备捕捉氧化物生长反应复杂性的能力，同时对于大规模扩展体系保持计算效率，为Fe-O体系的原子级模拟提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Accurate atomistic modelling of iron (Fe) oxidation requires a reliable interatomic potential, which necessitates an extensive and representative first-principles dataset for training the interatomic potential. However, Fe-oxygen (O) system is known for its structural and magnetic complexity, rendering the generation of high-quality dataset challenging. In this work, we propose the Perturbation Augmented Space group structure Sampling (PASS) method to generate extensive and representative dataset consisting of small-cell structures with less than 10 atoms. We present a systematic approach to developing a first of its kind transferable machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP) for Fe-O system based on the atomic cluster expansion (ACE) framework. We thoroughly validate the accuracy and capability of the ACE MLIP across both pure Fe and Fe-O systems through bulk, surface, and interface properties. We showcase the formation of FeO-like structure in large-scale Fe oxidation simulation using the ACE MLIP. This work demonstrates that the PASS method yields an accurate and transferable MLIP which is capable of capturing the reactive complexity of oxide growth while remaining computationally practical for extended systems.

</details>


### [151] [Unconventional and Fragile Magnetic Exciton in a van der Waals Quantum Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27695)
*Kai-Xuan Zhang,Min Zhang,Minjae Kim,Yong-Hyun Kim,Junghyun Kim,Heejun Yang,Pyeongjae Park,Chaebin Kim,Mangesh Diware,Junik Hwang,Youjin Lee,Byeong-Gwan Cho,Hyeong-Do Kim,Tae-Yeong Koo,Chunhua Chen,Mingtao Li,Xujie Lü,Wenge Yang,Kee-Hoon Kim,Seung-Ho Baek,Hyeonsik Cheong,Sung-Keun Lee,Beom Hyun Kim,Christopher Lane,Jian-Xin Zhu,Zhaorong Yang,Young-Woo Son,Je-Geun Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过静水压作为清洁、连续、可逆的原位调控手段，发现NiPS₃范德华反铁磁体中的磁激子光致发光峰在0.4 GPa即被显著抑制、1.5 GPa完全淬灭，且这种亮态到暗态的转变不伴随磁、晶体或电子结构重构。结果表明磁激子的光学亮度与化学无序、晶格膨胀和磁序减弱无关，暗示高阶关联机制（如激子配对、晶体场控制的旋轨耦合混合、对称性破缺）主导亮激子行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiPS₃中的磁激子具有尖锐且光学明亮的特性，尽管其跃迁名义上是自旋禁阻的，但这一现象长期未被合理解释。关键问题在于磁激子的明亮特性究竟来源于化学无序、磁序减弱、晶格修饰还是亮激子本身的内在不稳定性，解答这些问题将对理论模型施加严格约束。

Method: 采用静水压作为调控参数，结合光致发光（PL）谱、拉曼光谱、X射线吸收谱（XAS）、核磁共振（NMR）光谱以及第一性原理多体计算，系统追踪压力对磁激子光学性质及磁/晶格/电子结构的影响。

Result: 发现磁激子的光致发光峰在0.4 GPa下急剧抑制，1.5 GPa下完全淬灭且过程可逆。此亮-暗转变不伴随磁性、晶体或电子重构，且尼尔温度反而升高。这表明光学亮度与化学无序、晶格膨胀和磁序减弱均无关。

Conclusion: 磁激子的光学亮度由高阶关联机制控制，而非简单的化学或结构因素。作者提出包含激子配对、晶体场控制的旋轨耦合混合以及对称性破缺的微观场景，为后续相关量子磁体中纠缠磁激子的实验检验提供了框架。

Abstract: The recently discovered magnetic exciton in the van der Waals (vdW) antiferromagnet NiPS3 exemplifies these phenomena, exhibiting several distinctive characteristics. Despite extensive investigation, much of its physics remains unresolved, with key questions about why the NiPS3 magnetic exciton is so sharp and optically bright despite the nominally spin-forbidden transition, posing significant challenges to a proper understanding and practical manipulation of the exciton. An urgent question is to what extent it is due to chemical disorder, magnetic weakening, lattice modification, or intrinsic instability of the bright exciton itself: answers to which will put stringent constraints on possible theoretical models. Here we address these questions using hydrostatic pressure as a clean, continuous, reversible, and in-situ tuning parameter. We find that the sharp photoluminescence peak is drastically suppressed by as little as 0.4 GPa and completely quenched by 1.5 GPa, with demonstrating its reversibility. Crucially, this bright-to-dark conversion occurs without magnetic, crystallographic, or electronic reconstruction despite an increase in the Neel temperature, as established by Raman, X-ray absorption, nuclear magnetic resonance spectroscopy, and first-principles many-body calculations. Our results demonstrate that the optical brightness of the magnetic exciton is independent of chemical disorder, lattice expansion, and weakening of magnetic order, indicating that a higher-order correlated mechanism governs the bright exciton. We further propose experimentally constrained microscopic scenarios involving exciton pairing, crystal-field-controlled spin-orbit mixing, and symmetry breaking, providing a framework for future tests of entangled magnetic exciton in correlated quantum magnets.

</details>


### [152] [Optimization of magneto-electric properties in Lead-free (x)Co1.2Ti0.2Fe1.6O4 - (100-x)BaTiO3 based composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27717)
*Rajeev Dwivedi,Samanway Mohanta,Ashutosh Anand,Abhinash Tripathy,Najnin Bano,Dharmendra Kumar,Hemant Singh,R. Venkatesh,Sachin Gupta,Dinesh Kumar Shukla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无铅多铁复合材料(x)Co1.2Ti0.2Fe1.6O4-(100-x)BaTiO3的结构、电学、磁学及磁电耦合性能，发现30CTFO-70BTO在1200°C烧结时具有最高磁电系数(~1.28 mV/cm.Oe)，归因于磁致伸缩与压电响应之间的最优平衡及界面应变传递的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无铅多铁复合材料中组成与烧结温度对结构、电学、磁学和磁电性能的影响，以实现高性能的磁电耦合，为下一代低功耗多功能器件提供铅-free材料方案。

Method: 采用固相反应法合成了三种不同成分比例的CTFO-BTO复合材料（x=10, 20, 30），通过X射线衍射和Rietveld精修确认两相共存（四方BTO与立方尖晶石CTFO），并结合微观结构分析、介电/铁电测试、磁性测量及磁电系数测试系统表征材料性能。

Result: 所有复合材料均表现出依赖于组成和烧结条件的磁电系数；30CTFO-70BTO在1200°C烧结时获得最高磁电系数约1.28 mV/cm.Oe。高温烧结促进致密化和晶粒生长，改善两相间耦合；铁氧体含量增加导致磁化强度增大，介电/铁电行为呈现有损的P-E回线。

Conclusion: 同时优化掺杂改性组成和烧结条件对于改善块体多铁复合材料中的磁电耦合至关重要，该无铅体系在高密度非易失存储（FeRAM/MRAM）、磁传感器、自旋电子器件和致动器等多功能器件中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: This work presents a systematic study of lead-free multiferroic composites of (x)Co1.2Ti0.2Fe1.6O4 - (100-x)BaTiO3 (x = 10, 20, 30), which were synthesized by a solid-state reaction method to investigate the effects of composition and sintering temperature on their structural , electrical, magnetic, and magnetoelectric (ME) properties. X-ray diffraction along with Rietveld refinement confirms the coexistence of tetragonal BaTiO3 (BTO) and cubic spinel Co1.2Ti0.2Fe1.6O4 (CTFO) phases. Microstructural analysis shows that densification and grain growth are better at higher sintering temperatures, leading to better coupling between the two phases. Dielectric and ferroelectric studies indicate lossy polarization-electric field (P-E) behaviour due to leakage from the conductive phase, while magnetic properties show increased magnetization with increasing ferrite content. All composites exhibit ME coefficients, which depend on the composition and sintering conditions; the highest ME coefficient (~1.28 mV/cm.Oe) was observed for the 30CTFO - 70BTO composite sintered at 1200 °C. This improvement is due to the optimal balance between magnetostrictive and piezoelectric responses and improved interfacial strain transfer. These results demonstrate that simultaneous optimization of dopant-modified composition and sintering conditions is essential for achieving improved magnetoelectric coupling in bulk multiferroic composites. Moreover, the results demonstrate the potential of lead-free composites for multifunctional device applications in next-generation, low-power technologies, including high-density non-volatile memory (e.g. FeRAM/MRAM), magnetic field sensors, spintronic devices, and actuators.

</details>


### [153] [Intertwined magnetoresistance and Hall multifunctionality in a non-coplanar magnetic Weyl semimetal DyB4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28283)
*Long Chen,Yulin Shen,Songxue Chi,Seunghoon Song,Yang Zhang,Jian Liu,Haidong Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了DyB4（一种受挫稀土四硼化物）作为磁性Weyl半金属候选材料，首次在同一材料中实现了超大磁电阻、手征反常负磁电阻、由内禀Berry曲率和偏斜散射共同贡献的大反常霍尔电导，以及由标量自旋手性驱动的拓扑霍尔响应这四种磁输运功能的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常磁输运响应（轨道运动、动量空间拓扑、实空间自旋手性）在单一材料中的集成极为罕见，因为其底层物理要求往往相互竞争。作者旨在寻找一种能同时容纳多种磁输运功能的材料平台，以建立多功能的磁性拓扑材料。

Method: 结合中子衍射（揭示一系列场可调磁态，包括非共面自旋构型和PT对称破缺相）、第一性原理计算（识别费米面附近陡峭的线性色散和场致Weyl点）、以及磁输运测量（验证四种输运响应）多种实验与理论手段，系统研究了DyB4的磁结构与电子结构。

Result: 在DyB4中观测到四重磁输运功能组合：超大磁电阻、类手征反常负磁电阻、由内禀Berry曲率与偏斜散射协同贡献的大反常霍尔电导、以及标量自旋手性驱动的拓扑霍尔响应。这些功能源于巡游载流子与局域4f磁矩的截然不同但弱耦合的角色。

Conclusion: DyB4提供了一种4f电子平台，用于关联轨道输运、动量空间Berry曲率和实空间自旋手性，为多功能磁性拓扑材料的设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Anomalous magneto-transport responses provide complementary probes of orbital motion, momentum-space topology, and real-space spin chirality, yet their integration into a single material remains rare because their underlying requirements often compete. A promising materials-design strategy is to realize a magnetic Weyl semimetal that combines linearly dispersive high-mobility bands with tunable non-coplanar magnetism while limiting spin-dependent scattering. Here we identify DyB4, a frustrated rare-earth tetraboride, as a magnetic Weyl semimetal candidate that embodies this strategy and hosts intertwined magnetoresistance and Hall multifunctionality. Neutron diffraction reveals a sequence of field-tunable magnetic states, including non-coplanar spin configurations and PT-symmetry-broken phases. First-principles calculations identify steep linear dispersions and field-induced Weyl points near the Fermi level. Magneto-transport measurements establish a rare fourfold combination of extremely large magnetoresistance, chiral-anomaly-like negative magnetoresistance, large anomalous Hall conductivity arising from cooperative intrinsic Berry curvature and skew scattering, and scalar-spin-chirality-driven topological Hall responses. This multifunctionality arises from the distinct yet weakly coupled roles of itinerant carriers and localized 4f moments, which enable high-mobility transport, field-induced Weyl topology, and non-coplanar magnetism. DyB4 therefore provides a 4f-electron platform for correlating orbital transport, momentum-space Berry curvature, and real-space spin chirality, suggesting a route toward multifunctional magnetic topological materials.

</details>


### [154] [High-Field EPR/ENDOR of N/Be Centers for Defect Engineering in 6H-SiC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27862)
*Yuliya Ermakova,Ekaterina Dmitrieva,Margarita Sadovnikova,Fadis Murzakhanov,George Mamin,Sergey Nagalyuk,Evgeny Mokhov,Marat Gafurov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用W波段（94 GHz）连续波与脉冲EPR及ENDOR技术，系统研究了共掺氮和铍的6H-SiC单晶，鉴定了氮施主和铍受主在不同晶格位置的性质，确定了它们的相位相干和自旋-晶格弛豫时间，并通过ENDOR和TRIPLE谱揭示了局部硅/碳环境中高度离域的电子自旋密度及耦合核自旋子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳化硅（SiC）在功率半导体电子学中应用广泛，其自旋缺陷又使其成为量子技术的重要候选平台。为了充分发挥SiC在量子技术中的潜力，需要深入理解双杂质共掺杂体系（如氮和铍）中缺陷的微观电子结构、自旋动力学及电子-核相互作用。

Method: 采用连续波和脉冲电子顺磁共振（EPR）以及电子-核双共振（ENDOR）技术，在W波段（94 GHz，磁场3.4 T）下对共掺氮和铍（浓度10^18 cm^-3）的6H-SiC单晶进行高分辨率谱学研究；并通过TRIPLE共振谱验证不同配位球间的耦合核自旋子空间。

Result: 脉冲EPR识别出不同晶格位置的氮施主和铍受主，确定了它们的相位相干时间和自旋-晶格弛豫时间；ENDOR测量揭示了与局部硅和碳环境（包括远距离配位球）的电子-核相互作用，超精细结构显示超胞内高度离域的自旋密度；TRIPLE谱验证了不同配位球的耦合核自旋子空间。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在保持晶格结构特征的前提下，可以在SiC中实现具有不同功能角色的双杂质共掺，为SiC基量子技术的材料设计与应用提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Silicon carbide (SiC) in its various structural modifications is widely used in power semiconductor electronics, operating under extreme conditions of high temperature, high voltage, and intense radiation. The discovery of spin defects (S>0) with unique optical and coherent properties has further positioned SiC as a promising platform for quantum technologies. Here, we investigate a 6H-SiC single crystal co-doped with nitrogen and beryllium at concentrations of 1018 cm-3, using continuous-wave and pulsed electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) and electron-nuclear double resonance (ENDOR). To enhance spectral resolution, experiments were conducted in the W-band (94 GHz; B = 3.4 T). Pulsed EPR identified nitrogen donors and beryllium acceptors in various lattice positions, allowing for the determination of their phase coherence and spin-lattice relaxation times. ENDOR measurements elucidated the electron-nuclear interactions with the local silicon and carbon environment, including distant coordination spheres. The observed hyperfine structures indicated highly delocalized spin density within the supercell. The TRIPLE resonance spectra verify coupled nuclear spin subspaces from different coordination spheres due to defect spin density. These results demonstrate the feasibility of incorporating dual impurities with distinct functional roles while preserving the crystal lattice`s structural features.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [A Quantum Mechanical Approach to the Computation of Rovibrational Spectra of Diatomic Molecules in Strong Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27907)
*Nikhil Yenugu,Ashwani K Tiwari,Sangita Sen*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Wilson-Hamiltonian框架的基础上，将其推广到三维通用框架，用于计算强均匀磁场中双原子分子的振转光谱。研究者实现了电场依赖的电子和核哈密顿量，公式化并实现了电场四极矩跃迁矩积分，首次提供了适用于实验解释的¹H₂分子在极端磁场环境下的全量子力学振转光谱计算结果，揭示了键刚化、转动势垒出现、场诱导的态耦合/退耦和对称性破缺等物理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁天体物理天体（如白矮星、中子星）的观测光谱缺乏实验数据，需要高分辨率和可靠的计算光谱用于解释。现有方法难以处理非微扰的磁场与分子运动的耦合，需要发展适合任意场强下双原子分子振转光谱的全量子力学计算方法。

Method: 将此前在JCTC文章中实现和基准化的Wilson-Hamiltonian框架扩展为适用于强均匀磁场中双原子分子的三维通用框架。该框架包含场依赖的电子和核哈密顿量，完全非微扰地捕获粒子运动与磁场的耦合，适用于所有场强。同时公式化并实现了外静磁场中振转跃迁的电四极矩跃迁矩积分，生成遵从分子-场系统选择定则的谱线。

Result: 获得了¹H₂在极端磁场环境下的首个全量子力学振转光谱结果，精度适合实验解释。观察到随场强增加的谱线位移、分裂、合并、出现和消失等现象。将电子和核的贡献分别及联合研究，揭示了键刚化、转动势垒出现、场诱导的态耦合/退耦以及转动和振动态的对称性破缺等底层物理。

Conclusion: 该方法对高场光谱学和天体化学建模具有直接意义，既提供计算数据参考，也深化了对强磁场对分子电子结构和核运动光谱影响的理解。

Abstract: In the absence of experimental data for molecular spectra in strong magnetic fields, high resolution and reliable computational spectra are required for the interpretation of spectra collected from highly magnetic astrophysical objects. In this paper, we extend the Wilson-Hamiltonian framework, recently implemented and benchmarked by us (\textit{J. Chem. Theory Comput.}, \textbf{21}, 9753 (2025) ), to a general three-dimensional framework suitable for computing the rovibrational spectra of diatomic molecules in strong uniform magnetic fields. The field-dependent electronic and nuclear Hamiltonians capture full non-perturbative coupling between particle motion and the field making the method applicable to all field strengths. The electric quadrupole transition moment integrals for rovibrational transitions in external static magnetic fields are formulated, implemented, and computed to yield spectra which respect the selection rules of the molecule-field system. The spectral changes with increasing field strength such as shifting, splitting, merging, appearance and disappearance of peaks are noted. Contributions from electrons and nuclei are studied individually, as well as in unison to reveal the underlying physics such as stiffening of the bond, emergence of a rotational barrier, field-induced coupling/decoupling of states, and symmetry-breaking in rotational and vibrational states. These results provide the first fully quantum mechanical computational results for the rovibrational signature of $^1\mathrm{H}_{2}$ in extreme magnetic field environments with accuracy suitable for experimental interpretation. The methodology developed herein has direct relevance for high-field spectroscopy and astrochemical modeling, both for providing computational data as well as for understanding the spectral impact of strong magnetic fields on electronic structure and nuclear motion in molecules.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum Computing Enabled ab initio Molecular Dynamics Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28548)
*Susanta Das,Subhamoy Bhowmik,Zhen Li,Milana Bazayeva,Danil Kaliakin,Akhil Shajan,Kenneth M. Merz*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子-经典混合工作流，将基于LUCJ拟设的量子测量结果通过样本量子对角化(SQD)后处理用于从头算分子动力学(AIMD)，能够恢复行列式子空间并提供能量和分析核梯度。在气相基准中SQD与FCI在1 kcal/mol范围内一致，在显式溶剂QM/MM模拟中也保持该精度，首次展示了由量子电子结构引擎驱动的凝聚相QM/MM动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将当前含噪声中等规模量子(NISQ)硬件实际集成到分子动力学模拟中面临巨大挑战，尤其是精确能量与解析核梯度的获取。现有量子算法在凝聚相QM/MM框架下的应用缺乏系统性验证，需要探索一种既能在当前量子硬件上可行又能达到化学精度的工作流。

Method: 采用化学启发的LUCJ(Low-rank Unitary Coupled Cluster)拟设进行量子测量，将测量结果通过Sample-based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD)后处理以恢复关键行列式子空间，从中提取精确能量和分析核梯度。在STO-3G基组下以完整组态相互作用(FCI)为精确基准，分别进行气相AIMD和显式溶剂QM/MM动力学模拟验证。

Result: 气相基准中SQD复原的FCI能量和梯度误差在1 kcal/mol以内，AIMD轨迹稳定；在QM/MM模拟中，SQD与FCI的能量涨落和均方根梯度曲线吻合，径向分布函数再现了溶质-溶剂结构。

Conclusion: LUCJ+SQD被确立为将当前量子硬件集成到QM/MM分子动力学中的实用路线，提供了凝聚相QM/MM动力学由量子电子结构引擎驱动的早期演示。

Abstract: We demonstrate a quantum-classical workflow for ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) in which quantum measurements from a chemistry-inspired LUCJ ansatz are post-processed using Sample-based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD) to recover determinant subspaces and deliver energies and analytical nuclear gradients for dynamics. As an exact benchmark, we use full configuration interaction (FCI) in the STO-3G basis, enabling a direct assessment of the accuracy of SQD. In gas-phase benchmarks, SQD reproduces FCI energies and gradients to within 1 kcal mol$^{-1}$ of the FCI reference and yields stable AIMD trajectories. In explicit-solvent QM/MM simulations, SQD retains this agreement, matching FCI energy fluctuations and RMS gradient profiles and reproducing solute-solvent structure as quantified by radial distribution functions. Overall, these benchmarks establish LUCJ+SQD as a practical route for integrating current quantum hardware into QM/MM molecular dynamics and provide an early demonstration of condensed-phase QM/MM dynamics driven by a quantum electronic-structure engine.

</details>

### [157] [Observation of Moiré Time Crystal in Floquet-driven Rydberg Atomic Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27999)
*Shuai Shi,Dong-Yang Zhu,Yu Yang,Chu-Rong Pan,Jing-Wen Tang,Ya-Peng Zhang,Yan-Li Zhou,Wei-Tao Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Bang Liu,Dong-Sheng Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文首次实验实现了Moaré时间晶体——通过双色驱动场（两个不同频率）与长程里德伯相互作用及耗散的协同作用，在时间域中观测到梳状Moaré拍频图案，该图案由次谐波周期与基频叠加形成，并绘制了系统的相图，确认了其对抗激光失谐扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moaré时间晶体作为一种非平衡量子相，源于两个不同频率的相干干涉（其中至少一个是破缺对称性时间晶体的固有振荡），其标志性特征为超长拍频周期，对应空间晶格失配产生的Moaré条纹在时间域的映射。然而，至今尚未有实验实现，填补这一空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: 采用双色驱动场（包含两个不同频率）作用于具有长程里德伯相互作用和耗散的量子多体系统，通过两个失配驱动之间的干涉形成梳状Moaré拍频图案，系统中叠加了次谐波周期性和基频。通过改变其中一个驱动的频率，在频谱中观察Moaré图案的错位分布，并系统扫描实验参数以绘制相图。

Result: 成功实验观测到Moaré时间晶体，获得了独特的梳状Moaré拍频图案，该图案在频谱中随驱动频率变化而错位；实验绘制了系统相图，并确定了一个鲁棒区域，在该区域中Moaré时间序能够抵抗激光失谐的扰动。

Conclusion: Moaré时间晶体的实现提供了一个可控平台，用于探索涌现的慢-快动力学和合成时空对称性，并为在驱动量子多体系统中工程化复杂时间序开辟了新途径。

Abstract: A Moiré time crystal is a non-equilibrium quantum phase emerging from the coherent interference of two distinct frequencies, at least one being the intrinsic oscillation of a symmetry-broken time crystal. Its hallmark is an ultra-long beat period, reflecting a time-domain mapping of the Moiré fringes that arise from mismatched spatial lattices. However, to date, no experimental realization of such a Moiré time crystal has been reported. In this work, by applying a bichromatic driving field with two distinct frequencies, we demonstrate that the interplay between long-range Rydberg interactions and dissipation gives rise to a unique comb-like Moiré pattern characterized by a beat-note comb, which superimposes subharmonic periodicity and fundamental frequencies. This Moiré pattern formed by two mismatched drives is staggered in the spectrum as the frequency of one driver changes. We experimentally map the phase diagram of the system and identify a robust region where the Moiré temporal order persists against perturbations in laser detuning. The reported Moiré time crystal not only provides a controllable platform for exploring emergent slow-fast dynamics and synthetic space-time symmetries but also opens avenues for engineering complex temporal order in driven quantum many-body systems.

</details>

### [158] [Suppressed Quantum Effects of Weakly Coupled Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27313)
*Yunjia Bao,Dhong Yeon Cheong,Nicholas L. Rodd,Joey Takach,Lian-Tao Wang,Kevin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，对于弱耦合波（如轴子暗物质和引力辐射），即使在量子态下存在非经典效应，现实探测器也无法探测到这些效应。原因有二：一是探测器耦合的是粗粒化的"有效模"而非场的基本模，非经典效应被抹平；二是所有非经典效应都被弱耦合的额外幂次所压制。这些结论排除了从引力波观测建立引力量子化的提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密实验日益瞄准弱耦合波（包括轴子暗物质和引力波），这些波通常被描述为经典场，但它们可能存在于没有经典对应的量子态中。作者希望探究是否存在根本性障碍，阻止实验探测这些非经典效应。

Method: 作者通过了两个独立的论证：一，分析现实探测器与场的耦合方式，证明探测器探测的是粗粒化"有效模"而非基本模，导致非经典效应被稀释；二，通过一般的理论证明，展示弱耦合条件下所有非经典效应（包括正交分量统计、数统计、纠缠和退相干）都被耦合强度的额外幂次压制。并以轴子腔体光缝仪（haloscope）作为具体示例。

Result: 证明了两大障碍：粗粒化有效模式会抹除非经典效应；非经典效应被弱耦合的额外幂次抑制。虽然原则上可通过高度压缩的探测器态等量子资源克服，但所需参数远超当前实验能力。结论适用于多种超轻暗物质搜索，并排除了通过引力波观测证明引力量子化的提议。

Conclusion: 弱耦合场的非经典量子效应在原则上难以被现实探测器观测到，这不仅是技术限制，而是存在原理性的障碍。尽管原则上可借助量子资源克服，但当前能力远远不足，且该结论对暗物质探测和引力波实验都有深远影响。

Abstract: Precision experiments increasingly target weakly coupled waves, including axion dark matter and gravitational radiation. Such waves are commonly described as classical fields, yet they could exist in quantum states with no classical counterpart. We exhibit two severe obstructions to detecting nonclassical effects, both independent of the mode occupancy. First, realistic detectors cannot resolve the fundamental modes of a field; instead they couple to coarse-grained "effective" modes, which often washes out nonclassical effects. Second, all nonclassical effects are suppressed by extra powers of the weak coupling, making them much harder to detect than the waves themselves. We prove this in general, and explicitly show how the suppression arises for quadrature and number statistics, entanglement, and decoherence. The suppression can in principle be overcome given suitable quantum resources, such as highly squeezed detector states, but the required parameters are far beyond current experimental capabilities. We use the axion cavity haloscope as an explicit example, although our conclusions apply to many ultralight dark matter searches, and rule out proposals to establish the quantization of gravity from observations of gravitational waves.

</details>

### [159] [Nonlinear Fourier spectral signatures of rogue waves observed in Bose-Einstein condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27734)
*Zhihao Zhang,Yankai Huang,Tiantian Li,Denglong Wang,Jie Peng*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文利用基于非线性薛定谔方程和Zakharov-Shabat散射问题的非线性傅里叶变换（NFT），研究了具有消失边界条件的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）物质波系统中两类一阶怪波（RW）的非线性光谱结构，揭示了离散孤立子本征模的相干动力学是怪波形成的根本机制，并提出基于离散谱相位匹配的逆谱工程设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调制不稳定性为连续背景上的怪波形成提供了重要框架，但具有消失边界条件的BEC物质波系统中怪波的形成机制和非线性光谱结构尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，从非线性谱的角度理解此类系统中怪波的深层物理本质。

Method: 采用基于聚焦非线性薛定谔方程可积结构与Zakharov-Shabat散射问题的非线性傅里叶变换（NFT）方法，结合Darboux重构进行非线性谱分析，研究了高斯波包诱导的极端局域化事件和实验观测的Peregrine孤子两类一阶怪波。

Result: 研究发现：高斯初始态中增大初始宽度会导致离散特征值数量增加，从而实现从基本孤子和束缚态到圣诞树状怪波结构的转变；实验观测的Peregrine孤子中，局域扰动重塑离散谱构型和相位演化，使多个束缚孤子模实现相干聚焦；此外揭示了高阶怪波的谱机制，并提出了基于离散谱相位匹配的逆谱工程设计方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解和控制具有消失边界条件的物质波系统中的怪波形成提供了非线性光谱视角，确立了离散孤子模的相干动力学作为怪波形成核心机制的地位。

Abstract: Modulation instability provides an important framework for understanding rogue wave (RW) formation on continuous backgrounds. However, the formation mechanism and nonlinear spectral structures of RWs in Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) matter-wave systems with vanishing boundary conditions remain largely unexplored. Here, we employ the nonlinear Fourier transform (NFT), based on the integrable structure of the focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation and the Zakharov-Shabat scattering problem, to investigate two representative classes of first-order RWs in BEC systems. Through nonlinear spectral analysis and Darboux reconstruction, we demonstrate that both Gaussian-wave-packet-induced extreme localization events and experimentally observed Peregrine solitons are governed by the coherent dynamics of discrete soliton modes encoded in the nonlinear spectrum. For Gaussian initial states, increasing the initial width leads to an increasing number of discrete eigenvalues, resulting in a transition from fundamental solitons and bound states to Christmas-tree-like RW structures. For experimentally observed Peregrine solitons, localized perturbations reshape the discrete spectral configuration and phase evolution, enabling coherent focusing of multiple bound soliton modes. Furthermore, we reveal the spectral mechanism of higher-order RWs and propose an inverse spectral-engineering approach based on discrete-spectrum phase matching. Our results provide a nonlinear spectral perspective for understanding and controlling RW formation in matter-wave systems with vanishing boundary conditions.

</details>


### [160] [Mean-Field Theory of Chiral Active Model B: Arrested Coarsening and Chiral Fingering Instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27305)
*Kristian Blom,Uwe Thiele*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文推导并分析了手性伊辛模型的平均场理论，从微观自旋块的动力学出发，建立离散演化方程与连续活性场论，揭示手性偏置会导致各向异性粗化、近矩形畴形貌以及手性诱导的粗化停滞，并通过线性稳定性分析发现了手性指状不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解手性活性物质中边缘电流如何塑造凝聚体形貌，特别是手性偏置对相分离和粗化动力学的影响机制，为Wang、Pietzonka和Jülicher提出的手性伊辛模型提供理论框架。

Method: 从描述2x2自旋块顺时针/逆时针旋转的主方程出发，推导空间分辨平均磁化的离散演化方程；通过取连续极限获得具有Model-B型弛豫动力学加手性电流结构的活性场论；利用该连续理论对径向对称团簇进行线性稳定性分析。

Result: 离散层面显示手性偏置导致各向异性粗化、畴呈近矩形形状并出现手性诱导的粗化停滞；连续场论表明微观旋转偏置产生定位于界面的切向电流；线性稳定性分析识别出手性指状不稳定性，角向扰动被放大并最终导致径向不对称旋转态或无序态。

Conclusion: 手性活性系统通过微观旋转偏置产生的边缘电流是塑造宏观凝聚体形貌和动力学行为的关键机制，该工作为理解手性活性物质的非平衡相变提供了系统性的粗粒化理论框架。

Abstract: We derive and analyze a mean-field theory of the chiral Ising model recently introduced by Wang, Pietzonka, and Jülicher in "Edge Currents Shape Condensates in Chiral Active Matter", arXiv:2603.20064. Starting from the master equation for clockwise and counterclockwise rotations of 2x2 spin blocks, we first obtain spatially discrete evolution equations for the spatially resolved average magnetization. On this discrete level, we show that a chiral bias strongly affects phase coarsening: domains coarsen anisotropically, develop nearly rectangular shapes, and eventually display chirality-induced arrested coarsening. Taking the continuum limit of these equations yields an active field theory that has the structure of a relaxational Model-B-type dynamics supplemented by a chiral current that permanently drives the system out of equilibrium. The coarse graining explicitly shows how microscopic rotational bias generates tangential currents localized at interfaces. Using this continuum theory, we perform a linear stability analysis of radially symmetric clusters and identify a chiral fingering instability in which angular perturbations of the interface are amplified and eventually lead to radially asymmetric rotating states or disordered states.

</details>

### [161] [Information-driven stepping in dimeric transport motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27495)
*Antonio Patrón Castro,David A. Sivak*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个二聚体转运马达（分子马达）步进动力学的理论模型，其中两个马达头部经历布朗运动，其迁移率依赖位置随时间周期性切换，使得一次只有一个头部移动。模型通过消耗化学自由能产生定向运动，并在稳态下表征了平均速度、能量流和信息流。研究表明该马达作为纯信息引擎运作，仅在组件间转换信息而无能量交换。对于局域化切换，模型得到与实验观测一致的平均速度表达式，并捕捉到紧耦合马达的失速力特征。将模型粗粒化为单一机械自由度后产生二阶非马尔可夫动力学，可计算四种不同的驻留时间分布用于单分子实验直接观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二聚体分子马达（如驱动蛋白）如何在纳米尺度上实现高效、定向的步进运动，以及其热力学机制——特别是信息如何在马达组件之间被转换、马达是否以类似麦克斯韦妖的方式进行操作，从而阐明这些分子马达卓越性能背后的物理原理。

Method: 建立了一个两头部马达的理论模型，核心思想是：两个马达头部各自进行布朗运动，其迁移率（mobility）依赖位置周期性地切换，保证一次仅一个头部移动。模型通过消耗化学自由能产生定向运动，并在稳态下分析平均速度、能量流和信息流。模型进一步通过局域化切换假设推导出热力学一致的平均速度表达式，并利用粗粒化方法将模型简化为单一机械自由度的二阶非马尔可夫动力学，从而计算四种驻留时间分布。

Result: 模型在稳态下表征了马达的平均速度、能量流和信息流，证明马达作为纯信息引擎运作——仅转换信息而不交换能量。局域化切换情形下，模型得到的热力学一致的平均速度表达式能够复现实验观测行为，并捕获紧耦合马达的失速力特性。粗粒化后得到的非马尔可夫动力学可用于计算四种不同的驻留时间分布，这些分布可在单分子实验中直接验证。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示信息转换（通过隐式麦克斯韦妖机制）可能是二聚体分子马达卓越性能的底层物理基础，为理解分子马达的热力学和信息处理机制提供了新的理论框架，并为单分子实验提供了可检验的预测。

Abstract: Dimeric transport motors are nanoscale protein complexes that move along cytoskeletal filaments. Here we introduce a theoretical model for their stepping dynamics, in which the two motor heads undergo Brownian motion with mobilities periodically switching in a position-dependent manner so that only one head moves at a time. Through consumption of chemical free energy, this mechanism produces directed motion. We characterize at steady state the motor's mean velocity and its energy and information flows. The motor operates as a pure information engine, where only information is transduced between its components, without energy exchange. For localized switching, the model yields a thermodynamically consistent expression for the mean motor velocity that reproduces experimentally observed behavior, and captures the stall force characteristic of tightly coupled motors. Finally, coarse-graining the model to a single mechanical degree of freedom produces a second-order non-Markovian dynamics, from which we compute the four distinct dwell-time distributions that can be directly observed in single-molecule experiments. Our findings highlight how information transduction, via implicit operation as a Maxwell demon, may underlie the remarkable performance of these molecular motors.

</details>

### [162] [Design principles for energy dissipation in viscoelastic network metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27525)
*Niranjan Sarpangala,Sean Fancher,Prashant K. Purohit,Eleni Katifori*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图拉普拉斯算子的谱框架，用于高效分析粘弹性桁架网络的机械能耗散。该框架保留了每根杆件的完整连续动力学，同时将计算规模缩减为与节点数相关而非离散化单元数。研究发现：随机重新分布截面面积通常会降低耗散，而基于梯度的优化能够产生非平凡的最优架构，其形态由基材的固有衰减长度决定。靠近全局共振模式时，最优质量分布从驱动节点以衰减长度尺度递减，且在衰减长度较小时最优架构与边界条件无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络材料中的机械能耗散在减振和抗冲击等应用中至关重要，但传统有限元方法在大规模无序桁架网络中识别最优耗散架构的计算成本过高，需要更高效的框架来探索此类材料的耗散性能优化问题。

Method: 开发一种基于图拉普拉斯算子的谱框架来处理粘弹性桁架网络：每个杆件保留完整的连续体动力学（精确解），问题规模仅随节点数量增长而非单元级离散点；利用该框架研究截面面积重分布对能耗散的影响，并采用梯度优化方法寻找最优架构。

Result: 随机重分布截面面积通常使耗散低于均匀基线；梯度优化则产生非平凡最优架构，其形式由基材固有衰减长度主导。在接近网络全局共振模的驱动频率下，最优质量分布从驱动节点出发以衰减长度尺度递减；当衰减长度较小时，最优架构对边界条件不敏感。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了耗散长度尺度可能作为复杂无序架构设计的新原则，所提出的高效计算方法为在更大规模的结构中探索此类设计提供了有力的工具，并启发了未来基于耗散长度尺度的设计研究。

Abstract: Mechanical energy dissipation in networked materials is relevant for applications from vibration isolation to impact protection, yet identifying optimal dissipative architectures in large disordered truss networks is computationally prohibitive with conventional finite element methods. We develop an efficient graph Laplacian-based spectral framework for viscoelastic truss networks, in which the full continuum dynamics of each rod are retained exactly and the problem size scales with the number of joints rather than element-level discretization points. Using this framework, we investigate how redistributing cross-sectional areas within a network (without changing material composition) controls energy dissipation. We find that random redistribution typically reduces dissipation relative to a uniform baseline, while gradient-based optimization yields nontrivial architectures whose form is governed by the intrinsic attenuation length of the base material. Focusing on driving frequencies near a global resonant mode of the network, we show that the optimal mass distribution decays from the source (driven joint) with the attenuation length scale, and at small attenuation lengths the optimal architecture is independent of the boundary conditions. These results motivate future studies of dissipation length scale based design principles on more complex disordered architectures and provide an efficient computational framework for exploring such structures at scale.

</details>


### [163] [Partial vision leads to an unexpected emergent collective behavior in active aligning particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27819)
*Raúl Molina-Prados Lallena,José Martín-Roca,Chantal Valeriani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有两个非重叠角视锥的广义Vicsek模型，通过调控视锥孔径面积和前-后方向偏置，系统研究了受限感知和非互易交互对活性粒子集体运动相行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Vicsek模型假设各向同性交互场，但生物系统普遍存在有限感知范围和视野盲区，需要推广模型以更真实地描述受限感知下的集体运动行为。

Method: 提出具有两个非重叠角视锥的广义Vicsek模型，通过调控视锥孔径面积(α)和前后方向偏置(β)两个参数，并结合短程体积排除效应，系统研究非平衡相行为。

Result: 限制侧向视野面积会破坏全局序、移动临界噪声并诱导高密度行波带；打破前后对称会产生非互易交互：前向偏置视野驱动"跟随领头者"式强聚集，后向偏置对齐则稳定具有抑制密度涨落的均匀群聚态；短程体积排除显著影响紧密聚集相的结构完整性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了非互易感知、空间各向异性和物理约束在活性物质中的相互交织作用，为理解生物受限感知下的集体运动提供了新见解。

Abstract: The Vicsek Model represents a paradigmatic framework for understanding the collective motion of active aligning particles, traditionally assuming isotropic interaction fields. Inspired by biological systems characterized by limited perception and blind spots, we propose a generalized Vicsek model featuring two distinct, non-overlapping angular vision cones. We systematically investigate the non-equilibrium phase behavior of this system by tuning the aperture area (α) and the front-back orientation (\b{eta}) of the cones. Our results reveal that restricting the lateral vision area destabilizes global order, shifts the critical noise, and induces highly dense traveling bands. Furthermore, breaking the front-back symmetry introduces non-reciprocal interactions that profoundly alter the emergent spatial structures: forward-biased vision drives strong clustering through "follow the leader" alignment, whereas backward-biased alignment stabilizes an exceptionally homogeneous flocking state with suppressed density fluctuations. Finally, we incorporate short-range volume exclusion, demonstrating that the structural integrity of these novel tightly-clustered phases is highly sensitive to steric interactions. Our work provides new insights into the interplay between non-reciprocal perception, spatial anisotropy, and physical constraints in active matter.

</details>


### [164] [Phase transitions and microphases in elastomers. I. Emergence of stable domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27516)
*Manu Mannattil,Haim Diamant,David Andelman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出弹性质在调控微相分离中的关键作用，利用传统的弹性理论通过体积守恒引入非局部热力学-弹性耦合，解释了各向同性溶胀弹性体中相变温度和畴尺寸对刚度的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验表明弹性效应可用于控制溶胀弹性体中的微相分离，但缺乏统一的理论解释微相形成的机制。作者旨在用传统弹性理论说明微相分离是如何产生的。

Method: 通过体积守恒将热力学与弹性耦合，构建非局部的热力学-弹性耦合理论，利用传统弹性理论框架来解释各向同性溶胀弹性体中的微相分离现象，并与实验结果对照。

Result: 理论成功复现了各向同性溶胀弹性体中相变温度和畴尺寸对弹性体刚度的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 微相分离可由传统弹性理论通过体积守恒产生的非局部热力学-弹性耦合来解释；伴侣论文将进一步研究各向异性溶胀和非均匀弹性模量的影响。

Abstract: Elasticity often plays a key role in regulating phase separation in physical systems. Recent experiments have shown that elastic effects can be used to control microphase separation in swollen elastomers. Here, microphase separation arises from a mismatch between the characteristic length scales of elastic and thermodynamic interactions. In this first part of a two-part paper, we show that microphase formation in elastomers can be explained using conventional theories of elasticity through a nonlocal thermodynamic-elastic coupling arising from volume conservation. Our theory reproduces the observed dependence of phase transition temperature and domain size on elastomer stiffness in isotropically swollen elastomers. In the companion paper, we investigate the effects of anisotropic swelling and inhomogeneous elastic moduli.

</details>

### [165] [Unification of Quantum Graph Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28024)
*Gian Luca Spitzer*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子集合子集的新定义，并基于此统一了量子图性质的推广定义，恢复了已有的着色和连通分量概念，并为独立集和团提出了改进变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图论中许多性质（如连通分量、独立集）都基于顶点子集定义，但量子集合（quantum set）子集的自然概念过于严格，导致量子图性质的推广定义杂乱无章，同一概念常存在多种不等价定义，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 作者引入一种新的、自然且有充分动机的量子集合子集定义，并以此为基础，将经典图性质的集合论定义直接推广到量子图场景，建立统一的量子图性质定义框架。

Result: 新框架恢复了已有的着色（colouring）和连通分量（connected components）的量子定义；对于独立集和团，所提出的变体与现有定义不同，但解决了现有定义的一些反直觉特性；同时展示了如何在该框架中恢复一个重要的现有独立集定义。

Conclusion: 通过引入更灵活的量子子集概念，可以统一并澄清量子图性质的多种已有定义，既保留已有正确概念，又能修正现有独立集和团定义的缺陷。

Abstract: Many properties of classical graphs are defined in terms of subsets of the vertex set. Examples include connected components, which are subsets $X \subseteq V(G)$ such that $X \times X^c$ and $E(G)$ are disjoint, or independent sets, for which $X \times X$ and $E(G)$ are disjoint. Direct generalisations of these definitions to quantum graphs are difficult to achieve, since the natural notion of subsets of a quantum set is much too rigid. As a consequence, approaches to generalising these classical properties to the quantum setting have been eclectic. In some cases, multiple inequivalent definitions of the same notion are in use. We introduce a natural and well-motivated alternative definition of subsets of a quantum set. Building on this, we propose unified definitions of quantum graph properties as straightforward generalisations of the classical definitions. We recover this way the established notions of colourings and connected components. For independent sets and cliques, our approach suggests variations that diverge from existing definitions, but address some of their counterintuitive properties. We nevertheless show how to recover an important existing definition of independent sets in our framework.

</details>
